### direct-ja

```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama privada con los últimos cambios de la base y mantener un historial lineal, siempre que no hayas compartido esos commits. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial tal como ocurrió, porque reescribir commits publicados rompe el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠、有序但要先建立连接，UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但更轻快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local branch to tidy it before you share it, so history reads as a clean line. Merge when the branch is already shared or pushed, since rebasing rewrites commits others may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts
```

如果只想跑某个文件里的某个测试，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`），后面跟测试名的一部分：

```bash
bun test test/math.test.ts -t "adds numbers"
```

不想写完整路径也可以，`bun test math` 会跑所有路径里包含 `math` 的测试文件。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to durable storage before the change is applied to the main data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover committed work and roll back anything incomplete.
```

    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(function(v){
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }
  $('day').value = today();
  $('add').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var amt = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    items.push({ day: $('day').value.trim() || today(), name: $('name').value.trim(), amount: amt });
    save();
    $('name').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    render();
  });
  $('days').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    if (!row.classList.contains('armed')) {
      row.classList.add('armed');
      row.querySelector('.k-row-value').textContent = '再点一下删除';
      return;
    }
    items.splice(Number(row.getAttribute('data-i')), 1);
    save();
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>

  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>

</section>
<h2 class="k-h">今天的奶</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="如 14:30"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="如 100"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const d = new Date();
const TODAY = d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate());
let feeds = [], editing = -1;
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
const okTime = t => /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(t);

function sorted() {
  return feeds.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, i }))
    .filter(f => f.day === TODAY)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.time < b.time ? -1 : 1));
}

function editRow(f) {
  return '<div class="k-row">' +
    '<input class="k-input" id="e-t" value="' + f.time  … s="k-row-main">' + f.time +
      '</span><span class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</span></div>'
  ).join('');
}

function save() {
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  render();
}

$('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const s = e.target.closest('[data-save]');
  const x = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  const r = e.target.closest('[data-i]');
  if (s) {
    const t = $('e-t').value.trim(), ml = Number($('e-ml').value);
    if (!okTime(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    feeds[Number(s.dataset.save)] = { ...feeds[Number(s.dataset.save)], time: t, ml };
    editing = -1; save();
  } else if (x) {
    feeds.splice(Number(x.dataset.del), 1);
    editing = -1; save();
  } else if (r) {
    editing = Number(r.dataset.i); render();
  }
});

$('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const t = $('t').value.trim(), ml = Number($('ml').value);
  if (!okTime(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  feeds.push({ day: TODAY, time: t, ml });
  $('t').value = ''; $('ml').value = '';
  $('add').closest('details').open = false;
  save();
});

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(v => {
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"day":"2026-11-23","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"day":"2026-11-23","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, each with a tick box. Tick one when it lands, and the count of open ones updates. The three waiting on someone are at the top, the draft below them, and your ticks are saved between visits.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2041 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still open</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">3</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="waiting"></div></div>

  <h2 class="k-h">Draft</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="draft"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
  var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var landed = {};

  function save() { ducter.set('landed', landed); }

  function makeRow(it) {
    var on = !!landed[it.id];
    var row = document.createElement('label');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var cb = document.createElement('input');
    cb.type = 'checkbox';
    cb.checked = on;
    cb.onchange = function () {
      landed[it.id] = cb.checked;
      save();
      paint();
    };
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'k-row-main';
    var t = document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent = it.title;
    if (on) t.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
    var s = document.createElement('div');
    s.className = 'k-row-sub';
    s.textContent = on ? 'Landed' : it.sub;
    wrap.append(t, s);
    row.append(cb, wrap);
    return row;
  }

  function paint() {
    var waitingEl = document.getElementById('waiting');
    var draftEl = document.getElementById('draft');
    waitingEl.textContent = '';
    draftEl.textContent = '';
    var open = 0;
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
      if (!landed[it.id]) open++;
      (it.group === 'waiting' ? waitingEl : draftEl).appendChild(makeRow(it));
    });
    document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open ? 'Tick each one off as it lands' : 'All landed';
    ducter.summary(open ? open + ' open' : 'All landed');
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
      landed = v || {};
      paint();
    });
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();

### app-renew-zh

```
(earlier) user: 帮我订明天去北京的高铁，再订两晚酒店，离 agibar 近一点
(earlier) partner: 订好了：11/20 G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南，¥662；亚朵酒店（望京）11/20 和 11/21 两晚，¥1,036。
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 把「出差花销」换成新样子，能填的先帮我填好
  [partner message] 做好了，已经填上去程高铁 ¥662 和望京亚朵两晚 ¥1,036，合计 ¥1,698。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2317 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">这次出差花了</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips"><button class="k-chip" id="c1" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" id="c2" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div></details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
function pick(c){cat=c;$("c1").setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(c==="交通"));$("c2").setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(c==="住宿"))}
$("c1").onclick=()=>pick("交通");
$("c2").onclick=()=>pick("住宿");
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US")}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("total").innerHTML=xs.length?fmt(total):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记下第一笔，这里就会有合计";
  $("list").innerHTML=xs.length
    ?xs.map((x,i)=>'<div class="k-row" data-i="'+i+'"><div class="k-row-main">'+x.trip+' · '+x.cat+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+(x.day||"")+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+fmt(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join("")
    :'<div class="k-empty">这次出差还没有花销，记下第一笔吧</div>';
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"暂无花销");
}
$("list").onclick=e=>{
  const row=e.target.closest(".k-row");
  if(!row)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.splice(Number(row.dataset.i),1);
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  draw();
};
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value;
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value||"北京出差",day:$("day").value,cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让那个 TTL 任务在已有的测试里补一条正好到期那一刻的用例，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在 ~/dev/foo 的 ducter/foo-ttl 分支上补一条 TTL 测试，覆盖"正好到期那一刻"的行为：用假时钟把时间推进到恰好等于过期时刻（不早一毫秒，也不晚），断言此时值已经过期（取不到）。同时确认到期前一刻值仍然有效。保持假时钟，不引入真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过；把测试输出和连跑结果报告回来（连跑 20 次都通过）。

不做的事：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。在该分支上提交即可。
```
